On the next launch, the VM always starts from a cold boot.\n\nTo improve usability and align with modern VM workflows (e.g. UTM), we should provide a mechanism to **persist VM state (vmstate) to a local file** when the VM is suspended or the window is closed, and **automatically restore that state on the next launch.**\n\nThis would allow users to:\n\n- Close the VM window without losing runtime state\n- Resume the VM exactly where it left off\n- Avoid unnecessary reboot and reinitialization costs\n\nThe expected behavior is similar to **UTM-style suspend / resume**, where the VM memory and device state are serialized to disk and transparently restored on startup.\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
